import { createORPCClient } from "@orpc/client";
import { RPCLink } from "@orpc/client/fetch";
import { createTanstackQueryUtils } from "@orpc/tanstack-query";

import type { AppRouterClient } from "@my-app/api";

const link = new RPCLink({ url: `${window.location.origin}/rpc` });

const client: AppRouterClient = createORPCClient(link);

export const api = createTanstackQueryUtils(client);

import "@my-app/ui/globals.css";
import { RouterProvider } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { StrictMode } from "react";
import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";

import { createAppRouter } from "#src/router.tsx";

const router = createAppRouter();
const container = document.querySelector("#root");

if (!container) {
  throw new Error("Missing #root element");
}

createRoot(container).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <RouterProvider router={router} />
  </StrictMode>
);

import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createRouter } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import type { RouterHistory } from "@tanstack/react-router";

import { Loader } from "#src/components/loader.tsx";
import { createQueryClient } from "#src/lib/query-client.ts";
import { routeTree } from "#src/routeTree.gen.ts";

export const createAppRouter = (history?: RouterHistory) => {
  const queryClient = createQueryClient();

  return createRouter({
    routeTree,
    history,
    context: { queryClient },
    defaultPreload: "intent",
    defaultPreloadStaleTime: 0,
    defaultPendingComponent: Loader,
    scrollRestoration: true,
    Wrap: ({ children }) => (
      <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>{children}</QueryClientProvider>
    ),
  });
};

declare module "@tanstack/react-router" {
  interface Register {
    router: ReturnType<typeof createAppRouter>;
  }
}

    defaultPreload: "intent",

    defaultPendingComponent: Loader,
    scrollRestoration: true,
    Wrap: ({ children }) => <RegistryProvider>{children}</RegistryProvider>,
  });

  }

import { RegistryProvider } from "@effect/atom-react";
import { createRouter } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import type { RouterHistory } from "@tanstack/react-router";

import { Loader } from "#src/components/loader.tsx";
import { routeTree } from "#src/routeTree.gen.ts";

export const createAppRouter = (history?: RouterHistory) =>
  createRouter({
    routeTree,
    history,
    context: { session: null },
    defaultPreload: "intent",
    defaultPreloadStaleTime: 0,
    defaultPendingComponent: Loader,
    scrollRestoration: true,
    Wrap: ({ children }) => <RegistryProvider>{children}</RegistryProvider>,
  });

declare module "@tanstack/react-router" {
  interface Register {
    router: ReturnType<typeof createAppRouter>;
  }
}

import type { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { ReactQueryDevtools } from "@tanstack/react-query-devtools";
import {
  HeadContent,
  Outlet,
  createRootRouteWithContext,
} from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { TanStackRouterDevtools } from "@tanstack/react-router-devtools";
import { ThemeProvider } from "next-themes";

import { Toaster } from "@my-app/ui/components/sonner";

import { Header } from "#src/components/header.tsx";

interface RouterContext {
  queryClient: QueryClient;
}

const RootLayout = () => (
  <ThemeProvider
    attribute="class"
    defaultTheme="system"
    disableTransitionOnChange
    scriptProps={{ type: "application/json" }}
  >
    <HeadContent />
    <div className="grid min-h-svh grid-rows-[auto_1fr]">
      <Header />
      <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-2xl px-4 py-10 sm:py-14">
        <Outlet />
      </main>
    </div>
    <Toaster richColors />
    <TanStackRouterDevtools position="bottom-left" />
    <ReactQueryDevtools buttonPosition="bottom-right" />
  </ThemeProvider>
);

export const Route = createRootRouteWithContext<RouterContext>()({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [{ title: "my-app" }],
  }),
  component: RootLayout,
});

import { HeadContent, Outlet, createRootRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { TanStackRouterDevtools } from "@tanstack/react-router-devtools";
import { ThemeProvider } from "next-themes";

import { Toaster } from "@my-app/ui/components/sonner";

    attribute="class"
    defaultTheme="system"
    disableTransitionOnChange
    scriptProps={{ type: "application/json" }}
  >
    <HeadContent />
    <div className="grid min-h-svh grid-rows-[auto_1fr]">

# apps/web/src/routes/__root.tsx · 4 stacks
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-postgres-better-auth-docker-e2e
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-docker
#   spa-effect-http-effect-rpc-sqlite-better-auth-docker-e2e
import {
  HeadContent,
  Outlet,
  createRootRouteWithContext,
  useRouter,
} from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { TanStackRouterDevtools } from "@tanstack/react-router-devtools";
import { ThemeProvider } from "next-themes";
import { useEffect } from "react";

import { Toaster } from "@my-app/ui/components/sonner";

import { Header } from "#src/components/header.tsx";
import { authClient, getSession } from "#src/lib/auth.ts";
import type { Session } from "#src/lib/auth.ts";

interface RouterContext {
  session: Session | null;
}

const RootLayout = () => {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { session } = Route.useRouteContext();
  const current = authClient.useSession();
  const sessionId = current.data?.session.id;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (
      !current.isPending &&
      !current.error &&
      sessionId !== session?.session.id
    ) {
      void router.invalidate();
    }
  }, [
    current.isPending,
    current.error,
    sessionId,
    session?.session.id,
    router,
  ]);

  return (
    <ThemeProvider
      attribute="class"
      defaultTheme="system"
      disableTransitionOnChange
      scriptProps={{ type: "application/json" }}
    >
      <HeadContent />
      <div className="grid min-h-svh grid-rows-[auto_1fr]">
        <Header />
        <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-2xl px-4 py-10 sm:py-14">
          <Outlet />
        </main>
      </div>
      <Toaster richColors />
      <TanStackRouterDevtools position="bottom-left" />
    </ThemeProvider>
  );
};

export const Route = createRootRouteWithContext<RouterContext>()({
  beforeLoad: async () => ({ session: await getSession() }),
  head: () => ({
    meta: [{ title: "my-app" }],
  }),
  component: RootLayout,
});

import { getSession } from "#src/lib/auth.ts";

interface RouterContext {
  queryClient: QueryClient;
}

const RootLayout = () => (
  <ThemeProvider
    attribute="class"
    defaultTheme="system"
    disableTransitionOnChange
    scriptProps={{ type: "application/json" }}
  >
    <HeadContent />
    <div className="grid min-h-svh grid-rows-[auto_1fr]">
      <Header />
      <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-2xl px-4 py-10 sm:py-14">
        <Outlet />
      </main>
    </div>
    <Toaster richColors />
    <TanStackRouterDevtools position="bottom-left" />
    <ReactQueryDevtools buttonPosition="bottom-right" />
  </ThemeProvider>
);

export const Route = createRootRouteWithContext<RouterContext>()({
  beforeLoad: async () => ({ session: await getSession() }),
  head: () => ({
    meta: [{ title: "my-app" }],
  }),
  component: RootLayout,
});

#   spa-hono-orpc-sqlite-expo-docker
#   spa-hono-orpc-sqlite-expo-docker-e2e
import {
  useMutation,
  useQueryClient,
  useSuspenseQuery,
} from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Trash2Icon } from "lucide-react";
import { useState } from "react";
import type { SubmitEvent } from "react";

import { Button } from "@my-app/ui/components/button";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@my-app/ui/components/card";
import { Checkbox } from "@my-app/ui/components/checkbox";
import { Input } from "@my-app/ui/components/input";

import { api } from "#src/lib/api.ts";

const TodosPage = () => {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState({ title: "" });
  const { title } = draft;
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { data: todos } = useSuspenseQuery(api.todos.list.queryOptions());

  const invalidateTodos = {
    onSuccess: async () => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: api.todos.key() });
    },
  };

  const createTodo = useMutation(
    api.todos.create.mutationOptions(invalidateTodos)
  );

  const updateTodo = useMutation(
    api.todos.update.mutationOptions(invalidateTodos)
  );

  const deleteTodo = useMutation(
    api.todos.delete.mutationOptions(invalidateTodos)
  );

  const done = todos.filter((todo) => todo.completed).length;

  const handleSubmit = (event: SubmitEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    createTodo.mutate(
      { title },
      {
        onSuccess: () => {
          setDraft((current) => (current === draft ? { title: "" } : current));
        },
      }
    );
  };

  return (
    <section className="grid gap-6">
      <div className="flex items-end justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="grid gap-1">
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight">Todos</h1>
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
            Shared by everyone who opens the app.
          </p>
        </div>
        {todos.length > 0 && (
          <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm tabular-nums">
            {done} of {todos.length} done
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
      <Card>
        <CardContent>
          <form className="flex gap-2" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <Input
              aria-label="New todo"
              onChange={(event) => {
                setDraft({ title: event.target.value });
              }}
              placeholder="What needs to be done?"
              value={title}
            />
            <Button
              disabled={createTodo.isPending || title.trim() === ""}
              type="submit"
            >
              Add
            </Button>
          </form>
        </CardContent>
        {todos.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="grid gap-1 border-t px-4 pt-10 pb-6 text-center">
            <p className="text-sm font-medium">No todos yet.</p>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">
              Each one you add is saved through the API.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <ul className="-mb-4 divide-y border-t">
            {todos.map((todo) => (
              <li
                className="hover:bg-muted/40 flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2.5 transition-colors"
                key={todo.id}
              >
                <Checkbox
                  aria-label={todo.title}
                  checked={todo.completed}
                  onCheckedChange={(completed) => {
                    updateTodo.mutate({ id: todo.id, completed });
                  }}
                />
                <span
                  className={
                    todo.completed
                      ? "text-muted-foreground decoration-muted-foreground/50 flex-1 text-sm line-through"
                      : "flex-1 text-sm"
                  }
                >
                  {todo.title}
                </span>
                <Button
                  aria-label={`Delete ${todo.title}`}
                  onClick={() => {
                    deleteTodo.mutate({ id: todo.id });
                  }}
                  size="icon-sm"
                  variant="ghost"
                >
                  <Trash2Icon className="text-muted-foreground" />
                </Button>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        )}
      </Card>
    </section>
  );
};

export const Route = createFileRoute("/todos")({
  loader: async ({ context }) =>
    await context.queryClient.query(api.todos.list.queryOptions()),
  component: TodosPage,
});

import tailwindcss from "@tailwindcss/vite";
import { tanstackRouter } from "@tanstack/router-plugin/vite";
import react from "@vitejs/plugin-react";
import { defineConfig, loadEnv } from "vite-plus";

export default defineConfig(({ mode }) => {
  const { SERVER_URL = "http://localhost:3000" } = loadEnv(
    mode,
    import.meta.dirname,
    ""
  );

  return {
    plugins: [
      tanstackRouter({ target: "react", autoCodeSplitting: true }),
      react({ compiler: true }),
      tailwindcss(),
    ],
    server: {
      proxy: {
        "/api": SERVER_URL,
        "/rpc": SERVER_URL,
      },
    },
  };
});

#   spa-hono-void
#   spa-hono-void-e2e
import tailwindcss from "@tailwindcss/vite";
import { tanstackRouter } from "@tanstack/router-plugin/vite";
import react from "@vitejs/plugin-react";
import { defineConfig, loadEnv } from "vite-plus";

export default defineConfig(({ mode }) => {
  const { SERVER_URL = "http://localhost:3000" } = loadEnv(
    mode,
    import.meta.dirname,
    ""
  );

  return {
    plugins: [
      tanstackRouter({ target: "react", autoCodeSplitting: true }),
      react({ compiler: true }),
      tailwindcss(),
    ],
    server: {
      proxy: {
        "/api": SERVER_URL,
      },
    },
  };
});
